# Troika fixed machine gun: interactive demand and development acceptance

Date:2026-11-09. User has confirmed and authorized development. Recent access: A real machine gun handle is requested with empty hands; dimensions, angles and exposure range are specified by this session itself.[Public contracts](common.md)。

## 1 Operational objectives

The entry is requested without additional buttons or a press on Grip, with empty hands approaching the original gun position and touching the real machine gun handle; after confirmation of occupation, both hands continue to grab two grips, aim around the fixed tower axis and fire at the right trigger. Exiting from operation along the existing main line, restore free hand and normal body/vision point.

**Troika is not a gun that can be taken with him, and does not enter the shoulder sink, without changing boxes manually.**

## 2 Player Operating Steps

| Phase | Player Actions | It has to be done. |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Close | The empty-handed is on the way to position. | The physical machine gun handle must be touched by mere proximity to non-entry; conditions of original occupancy and character status maintained |
| Enter | Unarmed touch the real machine gun handle without pressing extra entry keys or Grip | One contact along the original claim ClaimTurret/DriverEnter; successful operation without VR occupancy and no frame-by-frame repeat request |
| Hold on. | Right and left hands, respectively, to the actual handle and to keep pressing LGrip/RGrip | Both hands get their current grips, and the model is reasonably exposed; no regular gun points pressed to hold. |
| Aim! | Two-hand move around the fixed axis. | Original parental stage-led artillery tower, compliance with original subsurface/level limit; free observation at head |
| Fire! | Hold R when double-handed.2 | Preheating/shooting and double-gun behaviour; no new single-handed mode |
| Hold your fire! | Matsuro.2 Or any Grip release/failed pursuit | Stop the original fire and remove hand-drive; distinguish between the status of the remaining gun position and the true exit |
| Catch and shoot. | Re-arrange the double handle, R.2 Let's unwind and then press it. | No automatic fire as a result of recapturing, no non-remedial firing during non-validity |
| Quit | Exit action using original main line position | Former Driver Leave/UnclaimTurret completed, hands free, sight point/mobility normal, no gun position occupancy and firing residue |

## 3 Original automated processes

Former CanReload = false, but automatically Reloating exists and may vary between particles, timings and Loader. Retain the original mechanism and actual example long; no status name can be seen to add a magazine or be changed to a custom calorie bar without proof.

Double-gun rounds rotated or were launched simultaneously, as was the case with the former InstantFire/Weapon Fired; VR only went in the opposite direction and from the legal original, without creating a central gun.

## 4 Feedback and body

The handshake/gun position hint distinguishes between entering, occupied, entering unshielded, double-sharping and re-emergence. Yellow only points to the current handle or step; it does not automatically suck a world gun slot into the player's hands.

The fixed axis and limit are derived from the actual gun position, which cannot be combined with long-shot/arms bones if there is a difference between the hands. The gun camera is separated from HMD observation and does not retain its mandatory orientation after withdrawal. The shoulder grab or bunker grabs are not assigned simultaneously during the time of the space occupation; the input is owned solely by the current context.

## 5 1. Must be accepted and accepted

1. Empty-hand handles unkeyd entry; only approaches, hold touch, miss jumps and drops.
2. The right hand and right handle correctly identified, the single-handedness of which cannot be used continuously; the ordinary gun RGrip rule is not violated.
3. Fixed axis, upper-down limit, left-to-west limit, only turning around without a gun; actual gun point is the same as the ray.
4. Original preheating, real double-gun shot and automatic resumption; Loader exists/does not maintain the original logic.
5. R-hold.2 Any Grip in possession ceases fire immediately; no automatic firing, Matsunaga 2 Re-press the validity.
6. Hands/gun positions/chips and firing are non-residual after withdrawal, failure to pursue, pause/chart, death/description.
7. Two-eye examination for handshake contact, hands posture, gun/body shield; recyclable sound and injury cessation confirmed separately.

## 6 Pending reconciliation and implementation portal

Access is defined by the user as "the handle of an empty hand-held machine gun" and is conditional on real, emptied, handle geometrical contact and access to the original gun position without waiting for additional key level confirmation. The exposure range, angle, axis and grip dimensions are considered/marked in this session; no old interactive buttons may be used as default entry prefixes. Exiting from existing primary line mapping is identified and recorded in this session; there is no conventional change of bullet and no new four seconds process is added.

The original fixed machine gun is separated from Junker's vehicle bearing, and is not replaced by its history.

## 中文

# Troika 固定机枪：交互需求与开发验收

日期：2026-11-09。用户已确认并授权开发。最新进入方式：空手触碰真实机枪柄即请求进入；尺寸、角度与接触范围由本会话自行标定。配套[公共合同](common.md)。

## 1. 操作目标

空手靠近原炮位并触碰真实机枪柄即可请求进入，无须额外按钮或先按Grip；原占用确认后，双手持续Grip抓两个握把，绕固定炮塔枢轴瞄准，右扳机开火。退出沿现有原主线操作，恢复自由手和正常身体／视点。

**Troika不是可随身拿走的枪，不进肩槽，不用手动换匣。**

## 2. 玩家操作步骤

| 阶段 | 玩家动作 | 必须结果 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 接近 | 空手正常走到炮位操作位置 | 仅靠近不进入，必须触碰实际机枪柄；保留原占用和人物状态条件 |
| 进入 | 空手触碰真实机枪柄，不按额外进入键或Grip | 一次接触沿请求原ClaimTurret／DriverEnter；成功才进入操作，失败不建立VR占用，不逐帧重复请求 |
| 抓握 | 左右手分别对准实际握把并持续按LGrip／RGrip | 两手取得各自当前抓握，模型接触合理；不沿普通枪点按保持 |
| 瞄准 | 双握移动，围绕固定枢轴改变方向 | 原父子层级带动炮塔，遵守原俯仰／水平限位；头显自由观察 |
| 开火 | 双握有效时按住R2 | 原预热／射击及双枪口行为；不新增单手模式 |
| 暂停射击 | 松R2或任一Grip释放／失追 | 停止拥有的原开火并解除手部驱动；仍处炮位的状态与真正退出区分 |
| 再抓再射 | 重新抓双握把，R2先松再新按 | 不因重新抓住而自动开火，不补发失效期间射击 |
| 退出 | 使用原主线炮位退出动作 | 原DriverLeave／UnclaimTurret完成，双手自由，视点／移动正常，无炮位占用和射击残留 |

## 3. 原自动流程

原CanReload=false，但自动Reloading存在，可能有粒子、计时及Loader差异。保留原机制和实际实例时长；不能看到状态名就加弹匣，也不能未经证明改成自定义热量条。

双枪口轮流或同时发射以原InstantFire／WeaponFired为准；VR只接对应方向与合法原起点，不自造一条中央枪口代替。

## 4. 反馈与身体

握把／炮位提示能区分可进入、被占用、已进入未双握、双握可射与原恢复中。黄色只提示当前握把或步骤；不能把世界炮位自动吸到玩家手边。

固定枢轴和限位来自实际炮位，双手不一致时不能拉长炮身／臂骨来拟合。炮位相机与HMD观察分开，退出后不保留强制朝向。炮位占用期间不同时分派肩取枪或掩体抓边；相关输入由当前上下文唯一拥有。

## 5. 必须验收

1. 空手触柄无按键进入；只靠近、持物触碰、失追跳变不进入。占用失败／已有其他占用不绑手，同次接触不重复请求；离开接触区后才能形成新的进入接触沿。
2. 左右握把正确识别，单双手缺失不能持续开火；普通枪RGrip规则不被破坏。
3. 固定枢轴、上下左右限位、仅转头不带炮口；实际枪口与射线一致。
4. 原预热、真实双枪口射击和自动恢复；Loader存在／不存在分别保留原逻辑。
5. 持R2松任一Grip立即停止拥有的开火；再抓不自动射，松R2重按有效。
6. 原退出、失追、暂停／剧情、死亡／读档后，手部／炮位／相机和射击无残留。
7. 双眼检查握把接触、双手姿态、炮体／身体遮挡；循环音和伤害停止分别确认。

## 6. 待核对与实施入口

进入已由用户定为“空手摸机枪柄”，实现以真实空手、手柄几何接触与原炮位准入为条件，不等待额外键位确认。接触范围、角度、枢轴与握把尺寸由本会话自行考虑／标定；不得用旧交互按钮作为默认进入前置。退出保留现有原主线映射，由本会话查清并记录；无常规换弹，不新增四秒流程。

复用 weapon_troika_interaction.h、原Turret_TroikaCabal／Troika_base的Claim／Unclaim／DriverEnter／DriverLeave／UpdateTurret及现有单写入后端。待核实际握把轴、枢轴、限位、双枪口、Loader和原恢复语义。固定机枪与Junker载具枪位分开，不以其历史通过替代。

来源：[W10](C:/Users/11/.codex/worktrees/6dof-single/gearsVr/docs/weapon-specs-20261028/weapon-cards.md#w10)、[原炮位入口](C:/Users/11/.codex/worktrees/6dof-single/gearsVr/docs/weapon-specs-20261028/original-entry-map.md)、[跨模块互斥](C:/Users/11/.codex/worktrees/6dof-single/gearsVr/docs/offline-tech-prep-20261028/shared-contracts.md)。



## 2026-11-09 实施检查点（未完成运行接线）

- 唯一工作区C盘6dof-single、分支codex/6dof-single；保留其他会话未提交改动，无新分支/worktree/助手，未操作共享Git暂存区或HEAD。
- TroikaInteraction进入改为空手触柄接触沿：无Grip/interact前置，同次接触只尝试一次；占用拒绝、持物、失追/连续性缺口后必须离开柄区再接触。原确认前hands保持await_tracking。左右柄分别要求有效接触处的新持续Grip；松手、失追或暂停恢复后旧Grip不能自动重获，R2必须真实松开再新按。
- 保留原动画交接，新增有实际原占用/原输入恢复证据的同步进入/退出回执；不虚构动画serial。新原适配weapon_troika_original.inl只准备CanEnterVehicle/TryToDrive，不独立重复ClaimTurret/DriverEnter；当前身份、原空手/仲裁、100ms请求期限、类型化反射参数与Driver/DrivenVehicle/PC.Pawn读回均检查。每个binding/token至多派发一次，未知结果保留责任；旧owner收尾不写新占用者。
